isr_adc_complete:

; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
; ADC interrupt dispatcher
; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
D612: 48         pha                                                       ; A-D conversion interrupt entry; saves registers then dispatches by ADC state flags
D613: 8A         txa
D614: 48         pha
D615: 98         tya
D616: 48         pha
D617: A5 F7      lda $F7

D620: A5 FA      lda $FA
D622: 48         pha
D623: A5 81      lda $81<ADC_Global_State>                                 ; ADC subdispatch; ADC_Global_State bit5=touch X phase, bit6=touch Y phase
D625: F0 0F      beq L_D636
D627: A7 81 1E   bbs 5,$81<ADC_Global_State>,adc_touch_x_dispatch
D62A: C7 81 21   bbs 6,$81<ADC_Global_State>,adc_touch_y_dispatch
D62D: 20 68 D6   jsr L_D668

L_D630:
D630: A5 81      lda $81<ADC_Global_State>
D632: D0 02      bne L_D636
D634: 9F 34      clb 4,$34<ADCON>

D639: 68         pla

D642: 68         pla

D644: 68         pla
D645: AA         tax
D646: 68         pla
D647: 40         rti

D648: 20 77 D8   jsr touch_x_adc_tick                                      ; touch X-phase ADC handler
D64B: 4C 30 D6   jmp L_D630

adc_touch_y_dispatch:
D64E: 20 97 D8   jsr touch_y_adc_tick                                      ; touch Y-phase ADC handler
D651: C7 81 11   bbs 6,$81<ADC_Global_State>,L_D665
D654: A7 80 0B   bbs 5,$80<ADC_State>,L_D662

touch_try_start_if_idle:
D83E: C7 D3 03   bbs 6,$D3<TouchFlags>,touch_try_start_after_flag_check    ; touch acquisition entry gate; starts only when the touch state is idle/enabled
D841: DF 80      clb 6,$80<ADC_State>
D843: 60         rts

touch_try_start_after_flag_check:
D844: A5 81      lda $81<ADC_Global_State>
D846: F0 01      beq touch_setup_x_phase_drive_x_read_an6
D848: 60         rts

touch_setup_x_phase_drive_x_read_an6:

; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Resistive touch acquisition - drive X, read AN6/P66
; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
D849: 3C 08 DA   ldm #$08,$DA<TouchSettleCountdown>                        ; TOUCH X phase: drive X rail, read AN6/P66 after settling
D84C: DF D3      clb 6,$D3<TouchFlags>
D84E: 4F 02      seb 2,$02<P1>                                             ; P1.2 high connects Touch_X- to GND
D850: 7F 02      clb 3,$02<P1>                                             ; P1.3 low connects Touch_X+ to VCC/PWR
D852: 4F D3      seb 2,$D3<TouchFlags>
D854: AF D3      seb 5,$D3<TouchFlags>
D856: DF 80      clb 6,$80<ADC_State>
D858: AF 81      seb 5,$81<ADC_Global_State>                               ; mark ADC_Global_State bit5 = touch X phase active
D85A: 6F D3      seb 3,$D3<TouchFlags>

touch_start_an6_conversion:
D85C: A9 06      lda #$06                                                  ; select ADC channel 6 (AN6/P66) and start conversion
D85E: 20 CD D7   jsr adc_start_channel_vref
D861: 60         rts

touch_setup_y_phase_drive_y_read_an5:

; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Resistive touch acquisition - drive Y, read AN5/P65
; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
D862: 3C 08 DA   ldm #$08,$DA<TouchSettleCountdown>                        ; TOUCH Y phase: drive Y rail, read AN5/P65 after settling
D865: 0F 02      seb 0,$02<P1>                                             ; P1.0 high connects Touch_Y- to GND
D867: 3F 02      clb 1,$02<P1>                                             ; P1.1 low connects Touch_Y+ to VCC/PWR
D869: 2F D3      seb 1,$D3<TouchFlags>
D86B: 8F D3      seb 4,$D3<TouchFlags>
D86D: CF 81      seb 6,$81<ADC_Global_State>                               ; mark ADC_Global_State bit6 = touch Y phase active
D86F: 6F D3      seb 3,$D3<TouchFlags>

touch_start_an5_conversion:
D871: A9 05      lda #$05                                                  ; select ADC channel 5 (AN5/P65) and start conversion
D873: 20 CD D7   jsr adc_start_channel_vref
D876: 60         rts

touch_x_adc_tick:

; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Touch ADC settle/final-sample handlers
; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
D877: 77 D3 0B   bbc 3,$D3<TouchFlags>,touch_x_final_sample                ; X-phase tick: discard settling conversions until countdown reaches zero
D87A: A5 35      lda $35<AD>                                               ; read and discard interim ADC sample
D87C: C6 DA      dec $DA<TouchSettleCountdown>                             ; decrement touch settle countdown
D87E: D0 02      bne touch_x_restart_conversion
D880: 7F D3      clb 3,$D3<TouchFlags>

touch_x_restart_conversion:
D882: 4C 5C D8   jmp touch_start_an6_conversion

touch_x_final_sample:
D885: 6F 02      seb 3,$02<P1>                                             ; final X-phase sample: disconnect X drive
D887: 5F 02      clb 2,$02<P1>
D889: 5F D3      clb 2,$D3<TouchFlags>
D88B: BF D3      clb 5,$D3<TouchFlags>
D88D: BF 81      clb 5,$81<ADC_Global_State>
D88F: A5 35      lda $35<AD>                                               ; read final AN6/P66 result
D891: 85 D0      sta $D0<Touch_RawAxis0_or_DBFE_anomaly>                   ; store raw first-axis sample
D893: 20 62 D8   jsr touch_setup_y_phase_drive_y_read_an5                  ; start Y phase immediately after X sample
D896: 60         rts

touch_y_adc_tick:
D897: 77 D3 0B   bbc 3,$D3<TouchFlags>,touch_y_final_sample_and_normalize  ; Y-phase tick: discard settling conversions until countdown reaches zero
D89A: A5 35      lda $35<AD>                                               ; read and discard interim ADC sample
D89C: C6 DA      dec $DA<TouchSettleCountdown>
D89E: D0 02      bne touch_y_restart_conversion
D8A0: 7F D3      clb 3,$D3<TouchFlags>

touch_y_restart_conversion:
D8A2: 4C 71 D8   jmp touch_start_an5_conversion

touch_y_final_sample_and_normalize:
D8A5: CF D3      seb 6,$D3<TouchFlags>                                     ; final Y-phase sample: disconnect Y drive and normalize coordinates
D8A7: 2F 02      seb 1,$02<P1>
D8A9: 1F 02      clb 0,$02<P1>
D8AB: 3F D3      clb 1,$D3<TouchFlags>
D8AD: 9F D3      clb 4,$D3<TouchFlags>
D8AF: DF 81      clb 6,$81<ADC_Global_State>
D8B1: A5 35      lda $35<AD>                                               ; read final AN5/P65 result
D8B3: C9 05      cmp #$05                                                  ; reject too-low Y sample
D8B5: B0 02      bcs L_D8B9
D8B7: A9 FF      lda #$FF

L_D8B9:
D8B9: C9 E4      cmp #$E4                                                  ; reject too-high Y sample
D8BB: 90 02      bcc L_D8BF
D8BD: A9 FF      lda #$FF

L_D8BF:
D8BF: 85 CF      sta $CF<Touch_Y_or_axis1_inv>
D8C1: 44 CF      com $CF<Touch_Y_or_axis1_inv>                             ; invert second axis so reported direction matches the other axis
D8C3: A5 D0      lda $D0<Touch_RawAxis0_or_DBFE_anomaly>                   ; load raw first-axis sample for thresholding
D8C5: C9 E4      cmp #$E4
D8C7: 90 02      bcc L_D8CB
D8C9: A9 00      lda #$00

L_D8CB:
D8CB: C9 05      cmp #$05
D8CD: B0 02      bcs L_D8D1
D8CF: A9 00      lda #$00

L_D8D1:
D8D1: 85 CE      sta $CE<Touch_X_or_axis0>
D8D3: 60         rts

touch_gate_validate_and_pack:

; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Touch validation and packet creation
; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
DB94: E7 CC 06   bbs 7,$CC,L_DB9D                                          ; touch packet gate: require touch enabled, request line, and nonbusy packet state
DB97: F7 D3 03   bbc 7,$D3<TouchFlags>,L_DB9D
DB9A: 37 08 01   bbc 1,$08<P4>,touch_start_or_check_packet_busy

L_DB9D:
DB9D: 60         rts

touch_start_or_check_packet_busy:
DB9E: 20 3E D8   jsr touch_try_start_if_idle
DBA1: A5 D8      lda $D8<TouchTxCountdown>
DBA3: D0 04      bne L_DBA9
DBA5: A5 D9      lda $D9<TouchTxIndex>
DBA7: F0 03      beq touch_validate_or_delta_filter

L_DBA9:
DBA9: 77 95 F1   bbc 3,$95,L_DB9D

touch_validate_or_delta_filter:
DBAC: 27 C2 09   bbs 1,$C2,L_DBB8
DBAF: 20 0D DC   jsr touch_delta_filter                                    ; optional delta filter before packet generation
DBB2: A5 CE      lda $CE<Touch_X_or_axis0>
DBB4: B0 20      bcs touch_pack_coordinate_packet
DBB6: 80 10      bra L_DBC8

L_DBB8:
DBB8: A5 CF      lda $CF<Touch_Y_or_axis1_inv>
DBBA: F0 0C      beq L_DBC8
DBBC: C9 FF      cmp #$FF
DBBE: F0 08      beq L_DBC8
DBC0: A5 CE      lda $CE<Touch_X_or_axis0>
DBC2: F0 04      beq L_DBC8
DBC4: C9 FF      cmp #$FF
DBC6: D0 0E      bne touch_pack_coordinate_packet

L_DBC8:
DBC8: 17 D5 D2   bbc 0,$D5<TouchPktHeader>,L_DB9D
DBCB: 17 C2 04   bbc 0,$C2,L_DBD2
DBCE: C6 D4      dec $D4<TouchHoldCounter>
DBD0: D0 CB      bne L_DB9D

L_DBD2:
DBD2: 1F D5      clb 0,$D5<TouchPktHeader>
DBD4: 80 24      bra touch_arm_bowman_int1_tx

touch_pack_coordinate_packet:
DBD6: 3C 00 D5   ldm #$00,$D5<TouchPktHeader>                              ; pack two 8-bit touch coordinates into a 3-byte Bowman/ASIC packet
DBD9: 0A         asl A                                                     ; move X high bit into packet header
DBDA: 26 D5      rol $D5<TouchPktHeader>
DBDC: 4A         lsr A
DBDD: 85 D6      sta $D6<TouchPktX_low7>                                   ; D6 = X low 7 bits
DBDF: A5 CF      lda $CF<Touch_Y_or_axis1_inv>                             ; load Y coordinate
DBE1: 0A         asl A
DBE2: 26 D5      rol $D5<TouchPktHeader>
DBE4: 4A         lsr A
DBE5: 85 D7      sta $D7<TouchPktY_low7>                                   ; D7 = Y low 7 bits
DBE7: 26 D5      rol $D5<TouchPktHeader>
DBE9: 26 D5      rol $D5<TouchPktHeader>
DBEB: 26 D5      rol $D5<TouchPktHeader>
DBED: EF D5      seb 7,$D5<TouchPktHeader>                                 ; set packet header/valid bits
DBEF: 0F D5      seb 0,$D5<TouchPktHeader>
DBF1: 3C 04 D4   ldm #$04,$D4<TouchHoldCounter>
DBF4: 3C 00 CE   ldm #$00,$CE<Touch_X_or_axis0>
DBF7: 3C 00 CF   ldm #$00,$CF<Touch_Y_or_axis1_inv>

touch_arm_bowman_int1_tx:
DBFA: 3F 3C      clb 1,$3C<IREQ1>                                          ; clear INT1 request and enable INT1 so Bowman/ASIC can request bytes
DBFC: 2F 3E      seb 1,$3E<ICON1>
DBFE: 3C 03 D0   ldm #$03,$D0<Touch_RawAxis0_or_DBFE_anomaly>              ; actual uploaded BIN writes #$03 to $D0; prior HTML labels this as $D8 - verify on hardware
DC01: 3C 00 D9   ldm #$00,$D9<TouchTxIndex>
DC04: 3C 04 99   ldm #$04,$99
DC07: 7F 95      clb 3,$95
DC09: 20 6F DC   jsr bowman_touch_tx_next_byte
DC0C: 60         rts

touch_delta_filter:
DC0D: A5 CF      lda $CF<Touch_Y_or_axis1_inv>                             ; touch delta filter; accepts coordinate changes inside X/Y movement thresholds
DC0F: F0 48      beq touch_clear_baseline_no_touch
DC11: C9 FF      cmp #$FF
DC13: F0 44      beq touch_clear_baseline_no_touch
DC15: A5 CE      lda $CE<Touch_X_or_axis0>
DC17: F0 40      beq touch_clear_baseline_no_touch
DC19: C9 FF      cmp #$FF
DC1B: F0 3C      beq touch_clear_baseline_no_touch
DC1D: A5 D1      lda $D1<Touch_prev_X>
DC1F: F0 2E      beq touch_delta_reject_update_baseline
DC21: 38         sec
DC22: E5 CE      sbc $CE<Touch_X_or_axis0>
DC24: F0 0C      beq L_DC32
DC26: 90 06      bcc L_DC2E
DC28: C9 1E      cmp #$1E
DC2A: B0 23      bcs touch_delta_reject_update_baseline
DC2C: 80 04      bra L_DC32

L_DC2E:
DC2E: C9 E1      cmp #$E1
DC30: 90 1D      bcc touch_delta_reject_update_baseline

L_DC32:
DC32: A5 D2      lda $D2<Touch_prev_Y>
DC34: 38         sec
DC35: E5 CF      sbc $CF<Touch_Y_or_axis1_inv>
DC37: F0 0C      beq L_DC45
DC39: 90 06      bcc L_DC41
DC3B: C9 28      cmp #$28
DC3D: B0 10      bcs touch_delta_reject_update_baseline
DC3F: 80 04      bra L_DC45

L_DC41:
DC41: C9 D7      cmp #$D7
DC43: 90 0A      bcc touch_delta_reject_update_baseline

L_DC45:
DC45: A5 CF      lda $CF<Touch_Y_or_axis1_inv>                             ; accepted: update previous coordinate baseline and return carry set
DC47: 85 D2      sta $D2<Touch_prev_Y>
DC49: A5 CE      lda $CE<Touch_X_or_axis0>
DC4B: 85 D1      sta $D1<Touch_prev_X>
DC4D: 38         sec
DC4E: 60         rts

touch_delta_reject_update_baseline:
DC4F: A5 CF      lda $CF<Touch_Y_or_axis1_inv>                             ; rejected/large jump: update baseline and return carry clear
DC51: 85 D2      sta $D2<Touch_prev_Y>
DC53: A5 CE      lda $CE<Touch_X_or_axis0>
DC55: 85 D1      sta $D1<Touch_prev_X>
DC57: 18         clc
DC58: 60         rts

touch_clear_baseline_no_touch:
DC59: A9 00      lda #$00                                                  ; no valid touch: clear previous coordinate baseline
DC5B: 85 D1      sta $D1<Touch_prev_X>
DC5D: 85 D2      sta $D2<Touch_prev_Y>
DC5F: 18         clc
DC60: 60         rts

isr_bowman_touch_request:

; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Bowman/ASIC touch packet output
; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
DC61: 48         pha                                                       ; INT1 handler from Bowman/ASIC: output next touch packet byte
DC62: 8A         txa
DC63: 48         pha
DC64: 98         tya
DC65: 48         pha
DC66: 20 6F DC   jsr bowman_touch_tx_next_byte
DC69: 68         pla
DC6A: A8         tay
DC6B: 68         pla
DC6C: AA         tax
DC6D: 68         pla
DC6E: 40         rti

bowman_touch_tx_next_byte:
DC6F: A5 D8      lda $D8<TouchTxCountdown>                                 ; byte transmitter: P0=data bus, P1.5 strobe pulse, D8=count, D9=index
DC71: F0 0F      beq bowman_touch_tx_idle
DC73: C6 D8      dec $D8<TouchTxCountdown>
DC75: A6 D9      ldx $D9<TouchTxIndex>
DC77: B5 D5      lda $D5<TouchPktHeader>,X
DC79: 85 00      sta $00<P0>                                               ; write packet byte to P0 bus toward Bowman/ASIC
DC7B: BF 02      clb 5,$02<P1>                                             ; P1.5 strobe low
DC7D: AF 02      seb 5,$02<P1>                                             ; P1.5 strobe high
DC7F: E6 D9      inc $D9<TouchTxIndex>
DC81: 60         rts

bowman_touch_tx_idle:
DC82: 3C 00 00   ldm #$00,$00<P0>
DC85: 3C 00 D9   ldm #$00,$D9<TouchTxIndex>
DC88: 60         rts
DC89: 4C 01 F7   jmp L_F701
DC8C: E1 58      sbc ($58<LCDRAM/RAM>,X)
DC8E: 01 D2      ora ($D2<Touch_prev_Y>,X)
DC90: DD 53 01   cmp $0153,X
DC93: C9 DD      cmp #$DD
DC95: 42         stp
DC96: 02 DB      jsr ($DB)
DC98: DD 54 07   cmp $0754,X
DC9B: 29 DE      and #$DE
DC9D: 57 08 9A   bbc 2,$08<P4>,L_DC3A
DCA0: DE 44 04   dec $0444,X
DCA3: 5F DF      clb 2,$DF
DCA5: 47 02 92   bbs 2,$02<P1>,L_DC3A
DCA8: DF 4D      clb 6,$4D<LCDRAM/RAM>
DCAA: 02 A7      jsr ($A7)
DCAC: DF 45      clb 6,$45<LCDRAM/RAM>
DCAE: 04         .db $04
DCAF: CE DF 46   dec $46DF
DCB2: 05 21      ora $21<TXH>
DCB4: E0 4B      cpx #$4B
DCB6: 01 54      ora ($54<LCDRAM/RAM>,X)
DCB8: E0 5A      cpx #$5A
DCBA: 04         .db $04
DCBB: 24 E1      bit $E1
DCBD: 52         .db $52
DCBE: 07 A9 E1   bbs 0,$A9,L_DCA2
DCC1: 43 08      bbs 2,A,L_DCCB
DCC3: AF E0      seb 5,$E0

L_DCC5:

DCC7: A5 AB      lda $AB
DCC9: 1A         dec A
DCCA: 1A         dec A

L_DD3D:

reset_entry:

; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Reset and initialization
; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
E6B1: 78         sei                                                       ; RESET vector target; CPU flags and MCU state are initialized here
E6B2: D8         cld
E6B3: 12         clt
E6B4: B8         clv
E6B5: 4C CC E6   jmp reset_init_start

E6B8: 20 E9 E7   jsr L_E7E9

E6BD: A9 00      lda #$00

E6C4: 4C 17 CD   jmp state_dispatch

E6C9: 4C 17 CD   jmp state_dispatch

reset_init_start:
E6CC: A2 00      ldx #$00

reset_sfr_init_loop:
E6CE: BC 1D E7   ldy $E71D,X                                               ; SFR initialization loop: copies address/value pairs from sfr_init_table
E6D1: C0 40      cpy #$40
E6D3: F0 0A      beq reset_clear_zero_page_ram

E6D6: BD 1D E7   lda $E71D,X
E6D9: 99 00 00   sta $0000,Y
E6DC: E8         inx

E6DF: A9 00      lda #$00
E6E1: A2 40      ldx #$40

L_E6E3:

E6E6: E0 00      cpx #$00
E6E8: D0 F9      bne L_E6E3

; Interrupt vectors

FFF4: AA E2      .word isr_serial_tx

VEC_SERIAL_RX:
FFF6: CF E2      .word isr_serial_rx

VEC_INT1:
FFF8: 61 DC      .word isr_bowman_touch_request

VEC_INT0:
FFFA: AF D1      .word isr_int0_power_lcd

VEC_RESET:
FFFC: B1 E6      .word reset_entry
